# Parity with RubyLLM 2.0, spec by spec

Generated by `bin/parity` from every `it` in `upstream/spec/ruby_llm/**/*_spec.rb` (372 files, 4658 examples, parsed with Prism by `bin/spec-examples`). Do not edit by hand: change `docs/parity-rules.txt` or cite the example from a test (`// spec: <file>:<line> <description>`), then rerun `bin/parity`.

| Status | Examples | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| PORTED | 2144 | a Rust test ports the example and cites it by file and line (or a rule names the test) |
| REPLAYED | 177 | a Rust test replays the example's own recorded cassette and asserts its request bodies |
| OWNED | 0 | not ported yet, and inside another in-flight agent lane (named) |
| N/A | 1479 | no Rust counterpart, with the reason (Ruby-only mechanism, or a provider this port leaves out) |
| MISSING | 858 | in scope for the port and not covered |
| **Total** | **4658** | |

N/A splits into 961 examples for providers this port leaves out (Azure, Bedrock, Vertex AI, Cohere, ElevenLabs, Deepgram, per BRIEF.md) and 518 with no Rust counterpart.
